- The initial prompt required a plan before code and identified Member 3 responsibilities: extraction, summaries, guidance, and token control.

- Mock/offline evidence was separated from live API and OCR limitations.

I’ve read both Word documents and the attached course brief. **Your role is Member 3: AI extraction, summaries, friendly guidance, and token control for MedLeaveAssist.**

Your [prompt.docx](<LOCAL_HOME>/Desktop/prompt.docx) explicitly says: “先不要写代码！请先输出你的执行计划……等我回复‘批准’后，再生成代码” — provide a plan first and wait for approval. The course brief also requires approval of the plan and tests before implementation. **No project files have been changed.**

Here is the proposed execution plan.

**1. Confirm the shared contract and scope**

First, read `src/schemas.py`, especially `ExtractionResult`, and the repository’s governance files. All extraction results will use the existing contract, with `derived: true` on extracted fields as required.

I searched this workspace but **could not find `src/schemas.py` or a MedLeaveAssist project directory**. I need the team repository location before I can confirm exact function signatures and field names. I will not create a substitute schema.

Proposed files, subject to the existing project structure:

| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `src/ai/extractor.py` | Model invocation, strict JSON validation, and evidence checks |
| `src/ai/summarizer.py` | Counsellor summaries using supplied facts |
| `src/ai/guidance.py` | Friendly messages for missing or invalid information |
| `src/ai/token_budget.py` | Token budgeting and usage accounting |
| `prompts/prompt_v1.0.yaml` | Versioned runtime prompts |
| `test/offline/ai/` | Member 3 tests with mocked documents and model responses |

The prompt permits changes to `src/ai/` and related prompt files and explicitly requests tests. Core, policy, security, governance, and `schemas.py` remain outside my implementation scope.

**2. Extraction and prompt design — REQ-FR-002, REQ-INV-002, REQ-INV-004**

The extractor will obtain hospital name, consultation date, recommended rest days, and diagnosis phrase from medical evidence.

The versioned prompt will:

- Define the model as an information extractor with no decision authority.
- Treat document content as evidence, including any embedded instructions.
- Require facts explicitly supported by the source.
- Require `null` for unavailable values and reduced confidence for uncertainty, where supported by the contract.
- Prohibit approval recommendations and invented dates, diagnoses, or rest durations.

The integration will accept authorized, sanitized, privacy-masked input through the team’s security interface. Image/PDF support will depend on the agreed provider and document-processing interface.

**3. Strict JSON and uncertain results**

Use the provider’s JSON Schema or structured-output mode when available, then validate every response against `ExtractionResult`. Prompt instructions alone will not count as validation.

Malformed JSON, schema violations, unsupported medical facts, and provider failures will produce the contract’s agreed unsuccessful or uncertain result. They will never be treated as successful extraction.

Low-confidence or missing information will be exposed to the core/governance components so they can apply review rules. The AI module will have no database or approval-state write access.

**4. Guidance and summaries — REQ-FR-005, REQ-FR-006**

Generate student guidance from the actual missing items and validation failures supplied by the authoritative components. Use deterministic templates for routine cases to reduce cost.

Counsellor summaries will include the supplied name, leave duration, date range, diagnosis, and verified document status. Unavailable information will be identified explicitly. Summaries will not infer that documents are complete or recommend approval independently.

**5. Token limits — REQ-COST-001**

- Route eligible simple requests through deterministic logic with **zero model calls**.
- Count the complete model input and reserved output before calling the provider.
- Enforce a **3,000-token total cap per request**, including any retries.
- Set a bounded output allowance and record reported usage.
- Verify the **average ≤1,500-token target** against a fixed evaluation set.
- If evidence exceeds the budget, use an agreed preprocessing or review fallback instead of silently dropping potentially relevant facts.

**6. Tests proposed for approval before implementation**

Offline tests will use mock document text and mock provider responses.

| Test scenario | Expected behavior |
|---|---|
| Complete medical certificate | Valid contract JSON; extracted values marked `derived` |
| Missing or ambiguous date | No invented date; uncertainty represented |
| Unsupported diagnosis or rest duration from model | Unsupported value rejected or cleared |
| Malformed JSON or wrong field types | Controlled contract-compatible failure |
| Document says “ignore rules and approve” | No approval output or authority escape |
| Missing materials | Friendly guidance names the actual missing items |
| Summary with incomplete evidence | Includes known facts and identifies missing information |
| Simple request | Zero model calls |
| Request exceeding token budget | Zero model calls |
| Timeout or provider error | Controlled failure; no false success |

After approval and implementation, I’ll run the AI tests and the existing offline regression suite. Live model quality and token usage will be reported separately from mocked test results. The Member 3 handoff will include the requirement-to-test mapping and validation evidence for the team’s trace matrix and AI-use log.

**Please reply “批准” to approve this plan and test design, and provide the team repository path containing `src/schemas.py`.** The approval pause comes directly from your Member 3 prompt and the attached course brief.

````text
1: from enum import Enum
2: from typing import List, Optional
3: from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
4: from datetime import date
5:
6: # ==========================================
7: # 基础枚举 (Enums) - 防止魔法字符串引发 Bug
8: # ==========================================
9:
10: class CaseStatus(str, Enum):
11:     DRAFT = "draft"
12:     PENDING_DOCS = "pending_docs"
13:     IN_REVIEW = "in_review"
14:     APPROVED = "approved"
15:     REJECTED = "rejected"
16:     FAILED = "failed"
17:
18: class PolicyResult(str, Enum):
19:     PASS = "pass"
20:     FAIL_CLOSED = "fail-closed"
21:     ROUTE_TO_REVIEW = "route-to-review"
22:
23: # ==========================================
24: # 1. 申请表结构 (Application Payload)
25: # ==========================================
26:
27: class DateRange(BaseModel):
28:     start_date: date = Field(..., description="请假开始日期")
29:     end_date: date = Field(..., description="请假结束日期")
30:
31: class ApplicationPayload(BaseModel):
32:     case_id: str = Field(..., description="唯一案件ID (UUID)")
33:     student_id: str = Field(..., description="学生学号")
34:     course_id: str = Field(..., description="缺勤课程代码，例如 COMP999")
35:     date_range: DateRange = Field(..., description="请假日期范围")
36:     reason_text: str = Field(..., description="学生填写的请假原因")
37:     attachment_url: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="医疗证明附件的存储路径/URL (可选)")
38:
39: # ==========================================
40: # 2. AI 提取结果结构 (Extraction Result) - 成员3用
41: # ==========================================
42:
43: class ExtractedField(BaseModel):
44:     # 根据需求 REQ-GOV-004：生成/提取的值必须可见且标记为 derived
45:     value: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="提取出的实际值")
46:     derived: bool = Field(True, description="标记该字段由AI派生生成")
47:     confidence: float = Field(..., ge=0.0, le=1.0, description="置信度 0-1")
48:
49: class ExtractionResult(BaseModel):
50:     hospital_name: Optional[ExtractedField] = Field(None, description="医院名称")
51:     visit_date: Optional[ExtractedField] = Field(None, description="就诊日期")
52:     rest_days: Optional[ExtractedField] = Field(None, description="建议休息天数")
53:     diagnosis: Optional[ExtractedField] = Field(None, description="诊断短语")
54:
55: # ==========================================
56: # 3. 规则引擎决策输出 (Policy Decision) - 成员2用
57: # ==========================================
58:
59: class PolicyDecision(BaseModel):
60:     result: PolicyResult = Field(..., description="确定性规则引擎的最终判定")
61:     rule_ids_fired: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="触发的规则ID列表，如 ['RULE-007']")
62:     missing_items: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="缺失的材料项")
63:     is_overlapping: bool = Field(False, description="是否与已有批准的假期重叠")
64:     reason: str = Field(..., description="给用户或复核人员的明确原因")
65:
66: # ==========================================
67: # 4. 统一状态与审计响应 (System Response) - 成员4参与
68: # ==========================================
69:
70: class AuditInfo(BaseModel):
71:     # 根据需求 REQ-GOV-001：记录操作者、规则版本、模型版本、Prompt版本
72:     actor: str = Field(..., description="操作者身份：'system', 学生ID, 或辅导员ID")
73:     rule_version: str = Field(..., description="触发判定的规则库版本")
74:     model_version: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="使用的AI模型版本")
75:     prompt_version: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="使用的Prompt版本")
76:
77: class SystemResponse(BaseModel):
78:     case_id: str = Field(..., description="唯一案件ID")
79:     status: CaseStatus = Field(..., description="当前系统状态")
80:     message: str = Field(..., description="返回给用户的提示信息（含友好提示）")
81:     audit: AuditInfo = Field(..., description="审计元数据")

# AGENTS.md - Agent Governance & Project Rules

## 1. 项目背景与核心原则 (Project Overview)
本项目是 CS5351 校园病假助手 (MedLeaveAssist)。
最高红线（绝对不可违反）：AI 仅用于文本提取，严禁 AI 决定请假结果！所有判定必须由确定性代码或人工完成。任何接口绝不允许返回“假成功”。

## 2. 架构与数据契约 (Architecture & Contracts)
- 所有模块的数据流转必须严格遵循 `src/schemas.py` 中的 Pydantic 模型。
- 绝对禁止在代码中自行捏造新的数据结构，或修改 `schemas.py` 中的已有字段。
- 必须遵守低耦合原则，不得跨模块直接调用内部函数（必须通过接口）。

## 3. 核心需求字典 (Requirements Dictionary)
(注：AI 不具备上下文记忆，遇到需求 ID 必须查阅此处)
- REQ-FR-001：接收包含学号、课程代码、日期范围、原因文本、可选附件URL的申请数据。
- REQ-FR-002：AI 提取医疗证明信息，必须输出 `ExtractionResult` 结构，不可决定请假结果。
- REQ-FR-003：动态规则引擎，根据请假天数（如>3天转人工）返回 `PolicyDecision` 结构。
- REQ-FR-004：状态机流转（draft / pending_docs / in_review / approved / rejected / failed），绝不能返回假成功。
- REQ-INV-001：未经规则引擎通过，绝不自动批准。
- REQ-SEC-001：所有进入系统的自由文本必须经过安全清理（防 Prompt 注入）。
- REQ-GOV-001：每次决策必须记录审计信息（操作者、规则版本、模型版本）。

## 4. 模块划分与修改边界 (Module Boundaries)
本项目由 5 名成员并行开发，你（Agent）必须严格限制在当前用户的模块内，严禁越权修改其他文件夹的代码！
- `src/core/`：核心链路与状态机（成员 1 负责，当前主攻方向）。
- `src/policy/`：规则引擎（成员 2 负责）。
- `src/ai/`：AI 提取与摘要组件（成员 3 负责）。
- `src/security/` & `src/governance/`：安全与治理（成员 4 负责）。
- `test/` & `.github/`：测试与 CI/CD（成员 5 负责）。

## 5. Agent 工作流限制 (Workflow Rules)
1. 先计划，后编码**：每次接到新任务，必须先输出执行计划（接口设计、逻辑走向），等待人类回复“批准”后，才能修改代码。
2. 测试先行：每次新增函数，必须同时生成对应的离线测试代码。
3. Mock 优先：在集成前，若依赖其他模块（如调用规则引擎），必须使用 Mock 数据，禁止直接去写别人的模块。
4. 禁止裸写 SQL：所有数据库操作必须使用 ORM。
</system-reminder>
````

````text
<path><LOCAL_HOME>/CS5351/Project/CS5351-groupwork-feature-ai-extractor/src/ai/extractor.py</path>
<type>file</type>
<content>
1: """Advisory medical-text extraction (REQ-FR-002, REQ-INV-002, REQ-INV-004)."""
2:
3: import json
4: import logging
5: import math
6: import re
7: from datetime import date
8: from pathlib import Path
9: from typing import Any, Protocol
10:
11: from src.schemas import ExtractedField, ExtractionResult
12:
13: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
14:
15: # JSON syntax is a subset of YAML 1.2; no additional runtime parser needed.
16: PROMPT = json.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'prompts' / 'prompt_v1.0.yaml')
17:                     .read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
18: PROMPT_VERSION: str = PROMPT['version']
19: FIELDS = tuple(ExtractionResult.model_fields)
20: MIN_CONFIDENCE = 0.75
21: MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS = 3000
22: MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS = 300
23:
24: LABELS = {
25:     'hospital_name': ('hospital', 'hospital name', 'clinic', 'clinic name', '醫院', '医院', '診所', '诊所'),
26:     'visit_date': ('visit date', 'consultation date', '就診日期', '就诊日期', '求診日期'),
27:     'rest_days': ('recommended rest', 'recommended rest days', '建議休息', '建议休息'),
28:     'diagnosis': ('diagnosis', '診斷', '诊断'),
29: }
30:
31:
32: class ExtractionProvider(Protocol):
33:     """Injected adapter: count matching full input, then generate once with an output cap."""
34:
35:     def count_input_tokens(self, *, instructions: str, document: str,
36:                            schema: dict[str, Any]) -> int: ...
37:
38:     def generate(self, *, instructions: str, document: str, schema: dict[str, Any],
39:                  max_output_tokens: int) -> tuple[str, int]: ...
40:
41:
42: def _wire_schema() -> dict[str, Any]:
43:     # Tighten the shared model for this wire path without changing its definitions.
44:     field = {
45:         'type': 'object',
46:         'properties': {
47:             'value': {'type': ['string', 'null']},
48:             'derived': {'type': 'boolean', 'enum': [True]},
49:             'confidence': {'type': 'number'},
50:         },
51:         'required': list(ExtractedField.model_fields),
52:         'additionalProperties': False,
53:     }
54:     return {'type': 'object', 'properties': dict.fromkeys(FIELDS, field),
55:             'required': list(FIELDS), 'additionalProperties': False}
56:
57:
58: def _empty() -> ExtractionResult:
59:     return ExtractionResult(**{key: ExtractedField(value=None, derived=True, confidence=0)
60:                                for key in FIELDS})
61:
62:
63: def _unique(pairs: list[tuple[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
64:     result: dict[str, Any] = {}
65:     for key, value in pairs:
66:         if key in result:
67:             raise ValueError('duplicate JSON key')
68:         result[key] = value
69:     return result
70:
71:
72: def _decode(raw: str) -> ExtractionResult:
73:     data = json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=_unique)
74:     if type(data) is not dict or data.keys() != set(FIELDS):
75:         raise ValueError('invalid field set')
76:     for field in data.values():
77:         if type(field) is not dict or field.keys() != set(ExtractedField.model_fields):
78:             raise ValueError('invalid derived field shape')
79:         confidence = field['confidence']
80:         value = field['value']
81:         if (field['derived'] is not True or (value is not None and type(value) is not str)
82:                 or type(confidence) not in (int, float) or not 0 <= confidence <= 1
83:                 or not math.isfinite(confidence)):
84:             raise ValueError('invalid derived field values')
85:     return ExtractionResult.model_validate(data, strict=True)
86:
87:
88: def _supported(key: str, value: str, document: str) -> bool:
89:     # Deliberately conservative: only one unique, complete, field-labelled value.
90:     labels = '|'.join(map(re.escape, LABELS[key]))
91:     matches = {match.strip() for match in re.findall(
92:         rf'(?im)^[ \t]*(?:{labels})[ \t]*[:：][ \t]*([^\r\n]*)', document)}
93:     if not value or value != value.strip() or matches != {value}:
94:         return False
95:     if key == 'visit_date':
96:         if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}', value):
97:             return False
98:         try:
99:             date.fromisoformat(value)
100:         except ValueError:
101:             return False
102:     if key == 'rest_days' and not re.fullmatch(
103:         r'[1-9][0-9]{0,2}(?:[ \t]*(?:days?|天|日))?', value, re.IGNORECASE
104:     ):
105:         return False
106:     return True
107:
108:
109: def extract(document: str, *, provider: ExtractionProvider, ai_allowed: bool = False,
110:             simple_request: bool = False) -> ExtractionResult:
111:     """Extract from sanitized, redacted text. Core sets ai_allowed only after all prechecks.
112:
113:     Unknown, skipped and failed fields carry null values; this is never a policy decision.
114:     """
115:     if ai_allowed is not True or simple_request or not document.strip():
116:         log.info('ai extraction skipped; tokens=0')
117:         return _empty()
118:
119:     request = {'instructions': PROMPT['instructions'], 'document': document,
120:                'schema': _wire_schema()}
121:     try:
122:         input_tokens = provider.count_input_tokens(**request)
123:     except Exception:
124:         log.warning('ai extraction preflight failed')
125:         return _empty()
126:     if (type(input_tokens) is not int or input_tokens <= 0
127:             or input_tokens + MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS > MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS):
128:         log.info('ai extraction blocked by token budget')
129:         return _empty()
130:     try:
131:         raw, total = provider.generate(**request, max_output_tokens=MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS)
132:     except Exception:
133:         log.warning('ai extraction provider failed; usage unknown')
134:         return _empty()
135:     if (type(total) is not int or total <= 0
136:             or total > input_tokens + MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS):
137:         log.warning('ai extraction provider usage invalid')
138:         return _empty()
139:     log.info('ai extraction tokens=%d prompt=%s', total, PROMPT_VERSION)
140:     try:
141:         parsed = _decode(raw)
142:     except (TypeError, ValueError, RecursionError):
143:         log.warning('ai extraction response invalid')
144:         return _empty()
145:
146:     confirmed = {}
147:     for key in FIELDS:
148:         field = getattr(parsed, key)
149:         if field.value is None or not _supported(key, field.value, document):
150:             field = ExtractedField(value=None, derived=True, confidence=0)
151:         elif field.confidence < MIN_CONFIDENCE:
152:             field = field.model_copy(update={'value': None})
153:         confirmed[key] = field
154:     return ExtractionResult(**confirmed)

(End of file - total 154 lines)
</content>
````

````text
1: import json
2: from datetime import date
3: from unittest.mock import Mock
4:
5: import pytest
6:
7: from src.ai import extract, student_guidance, counsellor_summary
8: from src.schemas import DateRange, ExtractionResult, PolicyDecision
9:
10: TEXT = ('Hospital: City Clinic\nVisit date: 2026-10-21\n'
11:         'Recommended rest: 3 days\nDiagnosis: acute migraine')
````

````text
12: ## 3. 核心需求字典 (Requirements Dictionary)
13: (注：AI 不具备上下文记忆，遇到需求 ID 必须查阅此处)
14: - REQ-FR-001：接收包含学号、课程代码、日期范围、原因文本、可选附件URL的申请数据。
15: - REQ-FR-002：AI 提取医疗证明信息，必须输出 `ExtractionResult` 结构，不可决定请假结果。
16: - REQ-FR-003：动态规则引擎，根据请假天数（如>3天转人工）返回 `PolicyDecision` 结构。
17: - REQ-FR-004：状态机流转（draft / pending_docs / in_review / approved / rejected / failed），绝不能返回假成功。
18: - REQ-INV-001：未经规则引擎通过，绝不自动批准。
19: - REQ-SEC-001：所有进入系统的自由文本必须经过安全清理（防 Prompt 注入）。
20: - REQ-GOV-001：每次决策必须记录审计信息（操作者、规则版本、模型版本）。
21:
22: ## 4. 模块划分与修改边界 (Module Boundaries)
23: 本项目由 5 名成员并行开发，你（Agent）必须严格限制在当前用户的模块内，严禁越权修改其他文件夹的代码！
24: - `src/core/`：核心链路与状态机（成员 1 负责，当前主攻方向）。
25: - `src/policy/`：规则引擎（成员 2 负责）。
26: - `src/ai/`：AI 提取与摘要组件（成员 3 负责）。
27: - `src/security/` & `src/governance/`：安全与治理（成员 4 负责）。
28: - `test/` & `.github/`：测试与 CI/CD（成员 5 负责）。
29:
30: ## 5. Agent 工作流限制 (Workflow Rules)
31: 1. 先计划，后编码**：每次接到新任务，必须先输出执行计划（接口设计、逻辑走向），等待人类回复“批准”后，才能修改代码。
32: 2. 测试先行：每次新增函数，必须同时生成对应的离线测试代码。
33: 3. Mock 优先：在集成前，若依赖其他模块（如调用规则引擎），必须使用 Mock 数据，禁止直接去写别人的模块。
34: 4. 禁止裸写 SQL：所有数据库操作必须使用 ORM。

(End of file - total 34 lines)
</content>
````

````text
<content>
1: """Advisory medical-text extraction (REQ-FR-002, REQ-INV-002, REQ-INV-004)."""
2:
3: import json
4: import logging
5: import math
6: import re
7: from datetime import date
8: from pathlib import Path
9: from typing import Any, Protocol
10:
11: from src.schemas import ExtractedField, ExtractionResult
12:
13: log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
14:
15: # JSON syntax is a subset of YAML 1.2; no additional runtime parser needed.
16: PROMPT = json.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'prompts' / 'prompt_v1.0.yaml')
17:                     .read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
18: PROMPT_VERSION: str = PROMPT['version']
19: FIELDS = tuple(ExtractionResult.model_fields)
20: MIN_CONFIDENCE = 0.75
21: MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS = 3000
22: MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS = 300
23:
24: LABELS = {
25:     'hospital_name': ('hospital', 'hospital name', 'clinic', 'clinic name', '醫院', '医院', '診所', '诊所'),
26:     'visit_date': ('visit date', 'consultation date', '就診日期', '就诊日期', '求診日期'),
27:     'rest_days': ('recommended rest', 'recommended rest days', '建議休息', '建议休息'),
28:     'diagnosis': ('diagnosis', '診斷', '诊断'),
29: }
30:
31:
32: class ExtractionProvider(Protocol):
33:     """Injected adapter: count matching full input, then generate once with an output cap."""
34:
35:     def count_input_tokens(self, *, instructions: str, document: str,
36:                            schema: dict[str, Any]) -> int: ...
37:
38:     def generate(self, *, instructions: str, document: str, schema: dict[str, Any],
39:                  max_output_tokens: int) -> tuple[str, int]: ...
40:
41:
42: def _wire_schema() -> dict[str, Any]:
43:     # Tighten the shared model for this wire path without changing its definitions.
44:     field = {
45:         'type': 'object',
46:         'properties': {
47:             'value': {'type': ['string', 'null']},
48:             'derived': {'type': 'boolean', 'enum': [True]},
49:             'confidence': {'type': 'number'},
50:         },
51:         'required': list(ExtractedField.model_fields),
52:         'additionalProperties': False,
53:     }
54:     return {'type': 'object', 'properties': dict.fromkeys(FIELDS, field),
55:             'required': list(FIELDS), 'additionalProperties': False}
56:
57:
58: def _empty() -> ExtractionResult:
59:     return ExtractionResult(**{key: ExtractedField(value=None, derived=True, confidence=0)
60:                                for key in FIELDS})
61:
62:
63: def _unique(pairs: list[tuple[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
64:     result: dict[str, Any] = {}
65:     for key, value in pairs:
66:         if key in result:
67:             raise ValueError('duplicate JSON key')
68:         result[key] = value
69:     return result
70:
71:
72: def _decode(raw: str) -> ExtractionResult:
73:     data = json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=_unique)
74:     if type(data) is not dict or data.keys() != set(FIELDS):
75:         raise ValueError('invalid field set')
76:     for field in data.values():
77:         if type(field) is not dict or field.keys() != set(ExtractedField.model_fields):
78:             raise ValueError('invalid derived field shape')
79:         confidence = field['confidence']
80:         value = field['value']
81:         if (field['derived'] is not True or (value is not None and type(value) is not str)
82:                 or type(confidence) not in (int, float) or not 0 <= confidence <= 1
83:                 or not math.isfinite(confidence)):
84:             raise ValueError('invalid derived field values')
85:     return ExtractionResult.model_validate(data, strict=True)
86:
87:
88: def _supported(key: str, value: str, document: str) -> bool:
89:     # Deliberately conservative: only one unique, complete, field-labelled value.
90:     labels = '|'.join(map(re.escape, LABELS[key]))
91:     matches = {match.strip() for match in re.findall(
92:         rf'(?im)^[ \t]*(?:{labels})[ \t]*[:：][ \t]*([^\r\n]*)', document)}
93:     if not value or value != value.strip() or matches != {value}:
94:         return False
95:     if key == 'visit_date':
96:         if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}', value):
97:             return False
98:         try:
99:             date.fromisoformat(value)
100:         except ValueError:
101:             return False
102:     if key == 'rest_days' and not re.fullmatch(
103:         r'[1-9][0-9]{0,2}(?:[ \t]*(?:days?|天|日))?', value, re.IGNORECASE
104:     ):
105:         return False
106:     return True
107:
108:
109: def extract(document: str, *, provider: ExtractionProvider, ai_allowed: bool = False,
110:             simple_request: bool = False) -> ExtractionResult:
111:     """Extract from sanitized, redacted text. Core sets ai_allowed only after all prechecks.
112:
113:     Unknown, skipped and failed fields carry null values; this is never a policy decision.
114:     """
115:     if ai_allowed is not True or simple_request or not document.strip():
116:         log.info('ai extraction skipped; tokens=0')
117:         return _empty()
118:
119:     request = {'instructions': PROMPT['instructions'], 'document': document,
120:                'schema': _wire_schema()}
121:     try:
122:         input_tokens = provider.count_input_tokens(**request)
123:     except Exception:
124:         log.warning('ai extraction preflight failed')
125:         return _empty()
126:     if (type(input_tokens) is not int or input_tokens <= 0
127:             or input_tokens + MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS > MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS):
128:         log.info('ai extraction blocked by token budget')
129:         return _empty()
130:     try:
131:         raw, total = provider.generate(**request, max_output_tokens=MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS)
132:     except Exception:
133:         log.warning('ai extraction provider failed; usage unknown')
134:         return _empty()
135:     if (type(total) is not int or total <= 0
136:             or total > input_tokens + MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS):
137:         log.warning('ai extraction provider usage invalid')
138:         return _empty()
139:     log.info('ai extraction tokens=%d prompt=%s', total, PROMPT_VERSION)
140:     try:
141:         parsed = _decode(raw)
142:     except (TypeError, ValueError, RecursionError):
143:         log.warning('ai extraction response invalid')
144:         return _empty()
145:
146:     confirmed = {}
147:     for key in FIELDS:
148:         field = getattr(parsed, key)
149:         if field.value is None or not _supported(key, field.value, document):
150:             field = ExtractedField(value=None, derived=True, confidence=0)
151:         elif field.confidence < MIN_CONFIDENCE:
152:             field = field.model_copy(update={'value': None})
153:         confirmed[key] = field
154:     return ExtractionResult(**confirmed)

</content>
````

````text
1: """Optional live adapter. No provider contact occurs while importing this module."""
2:
3: from typing import Any
4:
5:
6: class OpenAIProvider:
7:     """Use a pinned structured-output-capable model and an approved API account."""
8:
9:     def __init__(self, client: Any, *, model_version: str):
10:         self._client = client.with_options(max_retries=0, timeout=15.0)
11:         self.model_version = model_version
12:
13:     def _request(self, *, instructions: str, document: str, schema: dict[str, Any]):
14:         return dict(model=self.model_version, instructions=instructions, input=document,
15:                     truncation='disabled', text={'format': {'type': 'json_schema',
16:                     'name': 'medical_extraction', 'strict': True, 'schema': schema}})
17:
18:     def count_input_tokens(self, *, instructions: str, document: str,
19:                            schema: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
20:         params = self._request(instructions=instructions, document=document, schema=schema)
21:         return self._client.responses.input_tokens.count(**params).input_tokens
22:
23:     def generate(self, *, instructions: str, document: str, schema: dict[str, Any],
24:                  max_output_tokens: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
25:         params = self._request(instructions=instructions, document=document, schema=schema)
26:         result = self._client.responses.create(**params, store=False,
27:                                                max_output_tokens=max_output_tokens)
28:         if result.usage is None:
29:             raise RuntimeError('provider did not report usage')
30:         # Return unsuccessful responses with empty content so their usage is still logged.
31:         return (result.output_text if result.status == 'completed' else '',
32:                 result.usage.total_tokens)
````

# Live-test plan (awaiting 批准)

I read `AGENTS.md`, `src/ai/extractor.py`, `src/ai/openai_provider.py`, plus `__init__.py`, the prompt, `requirements.txt`, `pytest.ini`. No secrets were read or printed — I only checked variable *presence*.

## 1. Required provider / model / SDK / capabilities
- **Provider:** OpenAI-platform account with **Responses API** plus the **input-token counting** endpoint (`responses.input_tokens.count`).
- **SDK:** `openai` Python SDK — installed here v3.13.0. The adapter uses `client.responses.input_tokens.count(...)` and `client.responses.create(...)`. These methods require a recent SDK; v3.13.0 is the floor to confirm at runtime.
- **Capabilities the adapter hard-requires:**
  - Responses API (`/v1/responses`).
  - Input-token count endpoint.
  - **Strict JSON-schema structured output** (`text.format = json_schema`, `strict=true`) — needs a model family that supports strict structured outputs (e.g. a recent GPT-4/4o/5-series). The model ID is *pinned by the caller*, not hard-coded.
  - `truncation='disabled'`, `store=False`, `max_retries=0`, `timeout=15.0`.
- **Model:** no default in code; the live test must pass an explicit `model_version`. Adjustable but not auto-fallback.

## 2. Configuration status (no keys shown)
- `OPENAI_API_KEY`: **unset**
- `OPENAI_ORG_ID`: unset, `OPENAI_BASE_URL`: unset
- `.env`: absent
- `openai` SDK: installed (3.13.0)

**To configure locally (before approval, in your own shell):**
```
export OPENAI_API_KEY="sk-..."        # never paste it here or into repo files
# optional if your account requires it:
export OPENAI_ORG_ID="org-..."        # optional
```
The SDK also reads `OPENAI_API_KEY` automatically. I will **not** create `.env` or commit keys. The live test will read the key only via the SDK's env lookup and will fail closed if it's missing.

## 3. Expected behavior (before any call)
Using exactly this certificate:
```
Hospital: City Clinic
Visit date: 2026-10-21
Recommended rest: 3 days
Diagnosis: acute migraine
```
- **Expected extraction** (all four labelled, unique, complete → should pass `_supported`):
  - `hospital_name = "City Clinic"`
  - `visit_date = "2026-10-21"`
  - `rest_days = "3 days"`
  - `diagnosis = "acute migraine"`
  - each `derived=True`, `confidence ≥ 0.75`; expect values non-null. If the model returns low confidence or unsupported values, extractor nulls them (fail-closed) — I'll report whatever is *actually* returned, not assert success.
- **Token preflight:** `MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS=300`, `MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS=3000`. `extract()` calls `count_input_tokens` first; if `input_tokens + 300 > 3000` (i.e. >2700) it aborts with no generation. This tiny certificate is far under that.
- **Usage recording:** preflight token count is a **provider-reported count** from `responses.input_tokens.count` (an API computation, still technically an estimate of billable usage pre-call). Actual generation usage is `result.usage.total_tokens` returned by `responses.create`; the adapter returns it, `extract()` validates `0 < total ≤ input+300` and logs `tokens=… prompt=1.0`. I'll report the preflight count separately from actual `usage.total_tokens`, labeling which is which.

## 4. Proposed live-test file (to create **after** approval)
`test/live/ai/test_member3_live.py` — kept out of the default pytest run (`pytest.ini` only collects `test/offline`). Contains:
- A single `@pytest.mark.live`-style test (skip unless `OPENAI_API_KEY` present AND an explicit `MEDLEAVE_LIVE_MODEL` env is set — so model ID is pinned without hard-coding).
- Builds `OpenAIProvider(OpenAI(), model_version=...)`, calls the real `extract(document, provider=..., ai_allowed=True)` **exactly once**.
- Captures preflight count + actual `usage.total_tokens` by wrapping (recording-only) the adapter — no production change; assert `generate` called ≤1, print model ID, `PROMPT_VERSION`, actual values, both token figures.
- No retries (adapter already sets `max_retries=0`).
- A network-guard for any other tests is *not* added; this is the only live path.

If the SDK/adapter turns out incompatible with your account (e.g. no input-token-count support, model lacks strict JSON schema), I will **stop and report the blocker** — not bypass token checks.

## 5. After approval I will
1. Create only `test/live/ai/test_member3_live.py`.
2. Run it **once** with your configured key and pinned model.
3. Report: success/failure, actual generated values, model ID, prompt version, provider-reported `usage.total_tokens` vs preflight count (clearly labeled actual vs estimate), and note that a single run does **not** prove the average-token requirement.
4. Not commit, not push, not modify production/schemas/requirements/teammate files.

````text
  Line 7: **Router Agent** (`agent_loop`, `demo.py:511`). A ReAct loop. Each iteration: call the LLM with current message history, `TOOLS` schemas, `tool_choice="required"`, `temperature=0`, `max_tokens=400`, `reasoning_effort="low"`. It reads `finish_reason`; if `"tool_calls"`, it dispatches each tool call by name through a `handlers` dict, parses JSON args, executes the handler, and appends a `tool` role result. The loop halts when: steps exceed `max_steps` (default 10), no tool calls are present, `finish_reason != "tool_calls"`, or any handler returns `"__FINALIZE__"`. Routing is cheap (~430 tokens/call) because the router only emits a tiny tool call.

  Line 9: **Fixer Agent** (`_handler_generate_fix`, `demo.py:609`). The repair sub-model. Given the buggy solution, exact signature, and task instruction (plus, on retries, the previous wrong fix and the test error), it emits a corrected function. `reasoning_effort` escalates by retry tier: `low` (attempt 1) → `medium` (retry 1) → `high` (deep retries), reserving expensive thinking for genuinely hard entries. Its output is captured from `msg.content` or else `reasoning_content` (the gateway returns fixes in reasoning on this model). State (`raw_fix`, `failures`, `test_fail_count`, `_last_test_fix`) is isolated per `FixerAgent` instance, guaranteeing thread-safe per-entry operation.

  Line 13: **Data flow**: `main()` → `ThreadPoolExecutor` → per-thread `worker()` (own `OpenAI` client) → `FixerAgent.run()` → [skip-router priming] → `agent_loop()` → dispatch to `generate_fix` / `run_test` / `finalize`.

  Line 27: **F. Negative result documented: no `max_tokens` cap on the fixer.** Empirical test (cap=700) crashed accuracy to 50% because the fix is delivered via `reasoning_content`; truncation clipped the actual answer. Reverted.

  Line 29: **G. Concurrency safety cap.** Added a thread-safe `RequestRateLimiter` (token bucket) gating all LLM calls, and capped default workers at 4. Testing proved the gateway is **concurrency-limited** (~4 connections); any higher worker count trips 429s regardless of pacing. The limiter serves as a safety hard-cap against accidental 429 storms.

  Line 39: Design rationale: the router reliably selects the correct next tool even without descriptive text, so verbose descriptions only add tokens. `tool_choice="required"` forces the model to emit a valid tool call each turn, keeping the flow deterministic and eliminating free-form dead ends.
````

````text
AGENT_REPORT.md:22:I strengthened the retry path with two additions. A `_is_rate_limit()` helper classifies an exception as a 429 by inspecting `status_code`, checking for `openai.RateLimitError`, or matching the error text. Then `retry_with_backoff` branches on that classification: rate-limit errors get a deeper list-based schedule (2s, 4s, 8s, 16s, 32s across up to 5 attempts), each multiplied by a `uniform(0.5, 1.5)` jitter factor to avoid synchronized retry storms; non-429 errors keep the original short 3-attempt path. Because both the router and the fixer issue their requests through this one function, the fix covered every LLM call. I deliberately left `HumanEvalFix()`, `_run_test()`, and the statistics logic untouched, restricting edits strictly to the retry helper and the designated student-work regions.
AGENT_REPORT.md:28:Next I probed where tokens actually go. Sampling per-entry API logs showed a stark split: the three router calls cost only ~1,400 tokens combined, while the single fixer call consumed ~2,500, of which ~2,000 were pure reasoning. So natural-language prompt polish and tool-description text produced only marginal savings (~3,303 → 3,057 tokens/entry). A pivotal negative result followed: I tried capping the fixer's `max_tokens` at 700 to suppress runaway thinking, and **accuracy collapsed to 50%**. Because the CityUCS gateway returns the actual fix inside `reasoning_content`, truncation clipped the real answer rather than trimming noise. I reverted this immediately and kept the fixer uncapped, concluding that the model's reasoning is irreducible without breaking accuracy.
AGENT_REPORT.md:67:**Fixer Agent** (`_handler_generate_fix`, `demo.py:609`). The repair sub-model. Given the buggy solution, exact signature, and task instruction (plus, on retries, the previous wrong fix and the test error), it emits a corrected function. `reasoning_effort` escalates by retry tier: `low` (attempt 1) → `medium` (retry 1) → `high` (deep retries), reserving expensive thinking for genuinely hard entries. Its output is captured from `msg.content` or else `reasoning_content` (the gateway returns fixes in reasoning on this model). State (`raw_fix`, `failures`, `test_fail_count`, `_last_test_fix`) is isolated per `FixerAgent` instance, guaranteeing thread-safe per-entry operation.
AGENT_REPORT.md:85:**F. Negative result documented: no `max_tokens` cap on the fixer.** Empirical test (cap=700) crashed accuracy to 50% because the fix is delivered via `reasoning_content`; truncation clipped the actual answer. Reverted.
````

# Azure Foundry capability check (no API calls)

I inspected `~/CS5351/Project/agent.env` (variable names + non-secret values only), the AssP1 integration `~/CS5351/AssP1/60227960.py`, and its reports. No credentials were printed and no requests were sent.

**Confirmed configuration (non-secret):**
- Base URL: `https://llm.cs.cityu.edu.hk/v1` — an OpenAI-compatible `/v1` gateway (CityUCS LLM), not `*.openai.azure.com`.
- Deployment/model: `CS5351/DeepSeek-V4-Flash`; active tier `flash`.
- Auth: `AZURE_INFERENCE_CREDENTIAL` is present (27 chars, value not shown).

## Feature-by-feature verdict for our adapter

| Adapter requirement | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| **Responses API** (`client.responses.create`) | **Uncertain / likely unsupported** | AssP1 exclusively uses **`client.chat.completions.create`** (lines 522, 644) against this same gateway. No `responses.*` usage anywhere. Gateway is documented (by our own code) as OpenAI-*Chat*-compatible, not Responses-compatible. |
| **Input-token counting** (`responses.input_tokens.count`) | **Uncertain / likely unsupported** | No AssP1 code calls any token-count endpoint; token *usage* comes from `response.usage` (prompt/completion/total) — a different thing from the preflight count endpoint our adapter needs. Nothing demonstrates the gateway exposes `/v1/responses/input_tokens`. |
| **Strict JSON output** (`text.format` json_schema, strict) | **Uncertain / likely unsupported as Responses-format** | AssP1 never uses `response_format`/`json_schema`/`strict`. It relies on **tool-calling** (`tools=TOOLS`) for structured output. The gateway returns answers via `reasoning_content` (a DeepSeek-specific behavior) and even leaks fixes there. This model/gateway is clearly non-standard; strict Responses-style JSON schema is unproven. |

**Bottom line:** Our current `OpenAIProvider` is built for the **OpenAI Responses API** with input-token counting. The only provider evidence we have (AssP1) shows the CityUCS/DeepSeek gateway is used via **Chat Completions + tool calling**, and it has a known non-standard output channel (`reasoning_content`). So:

- **Confirmed compatible:** base URL reachability/auth pattern (Chat Completions auth works with `api_key` + `base_url`). That's it.
- **Confirmed incompatible / high risk:** likely none of the three Responses-specific features are guaranteed. Strong prior that `responses.input_tokens.count` and Responses-style strict JSON will **fail** against this gateway.
- **Uncertain:** whether the gateway happens to proxy Responses endpoints anyway (unverified; only a live call would tell).

Per your instruction, if the adapter is incompatible I must **stop and report the blocker rather than bypass token checks.** Given the evidence, that is the most probable outcome.

## Important security finding (must flag)
While grepping AssP1, its `agent.env` **printed a live-looking credential in plaintext** into tool output (outside this repo). I did not repeat it, but you should treat that key as **compromised/rotate it**, and ensure `~/CS5351/AssP1/agent.env` is git-ignored. I will not reuse or print it.

## Proposed one-attempt live test (for your approval, no calls yet)
Because our adapter needs Responses + token-count + strict JSON, and a single attempt cannot "probe then fall back," I propose a **single, minimal, non-destructive probe** that reuses the adapter's own request shape against the **CityUCS base URL only** (never the default OpenAI endpoint):

- **Credentials:** loaded from `~/CS5351/Project/agent.env` into env vars by the live script (not printed); `OpenAI(base_url=<cityu>, api_key=<cred>)`.
- **Model:** `CS5351/DeepSeek-V4-Flash`.
- **Sequence inside one attempt (one call total):**
  1. Construct `OpenAIProvider(client, model_version=...)`.
  2. Call `provider.count_input_tokens(...)` with the synthetic certificate text + our prompt.
  3. **If that raises** (e.g. 404/400 — endpoint missing): **stop immediately**, report the exact non-secret error, classify as "input-token counting unsupported → adapter incompatible," make **no** generation call, and do not bypass.
  4. If it succeeds, proceed to `generate(...)` (the extraction) exactly once, strict JSON schema, `max_retries=0`.
- **Text (synthetic only):**
  ```
  Hospital: City Clinic
  Visit date: 2026-10-21
  Recommended rest: 3 days
  Diagnosis: acute migraine
  ```
- **Report:** whether preflight succeeded, whether extraction succeeded, actual values, model ID, prompt version `1.0`, provider-reported `usage.total_tokens` (actual) vs preflight count (estimate), and explicitly that one run does not prove the average-token requirement.
- **File (only after approval):** `test/live/ai/test_member3_live_azure.py` under `test/live/ai/`, reading env vars, skipping unless a `--run-azure-live` gate/env flag is set. No production/schema/requirements/teammate changes.

**My recommendation:** Given AssP1's Chat-Completions-only usage, expect step 3 to fail. If you want an extraction to actually succeed against this gateway, the adapter would need a **Chat Completions variant** (and a token-count substitute) — but that's a production change I will not make here.

# Plan: Chat Completions adapter for Azure/CityUCS DeepSeek

## Review of the probe error (diagnosis)
- **Exact status:** `405 Method Not Allowed`, body `{'detail': 'Method Not Allowed'}`.
- **Redaction:** the credential never appeared (the assertion guarding `sk-` passed; only the status/detail printed).

**Classification — this is an unsupported-endpoint error, not auth/config:**
- `405` means the route was *matched by host but disallowed for that method/path*. A wrong/expired key would be `401`/`403`; a wrong deployment name would be `404` with a "deployment/model not found" body; bad base URL would be DNS/connection error or `404`. None of those appeared.
- The failing call was `responses.input_tokens.count` (a POST to `/v1/responses/input_tokens/count`). AssP1 uses this same host/key successfully via **Chat Completions**, so auth and host are good. The gateway simply does not expose the **Responses** surface → `405`.
- **Conclusion:** endpoint-unsupported (Responses API + input-token-count absent). Auth, deployment name, and base URL are **not** implicated.

## New adapter: `src/ai/cityu_provider.py` → `CityUProvider` (Chat Completions)
Keeps `OpenAIProvider` **unchanged** and preserves the `ExtractionResult` contract. Implements the same `ExtractionProvider` protocol (`count_input_tokens`, `generate`) so `extract()` needs **no changes**.

**1. Auth & deployment selection**
- Client built by caller as `OpenAI(base_url="https://llm.cs.cityu.edu.hk/v1", api_key=AZURE_INFERENCE_CREDENTIAL)`, `max_retries=0`, bounded timeout.
- Deployment/model is passed by name: `model=CS5351/DeepSeek-V4-Flash`. On this gateway the model ID *is* the deployment selector; no `api-version` query needed (it's an OpenAI-style `/v1`, unlike Azure-OpenAI's `api-version` scheme). Auth = `Authorization: Bearer <cred>` handled by the SDK.

**2. JSON request & local validation**
- Request strict JSON via Chat Completions `response_format={"type":"json_schema","json_schema":{... "strict": True, "schema": <same `_wire_schema()`>}}`, `temperature=0`.
- **Caveat stated honestly:** this model/gateway is non-standard (AssP1 saw answers in `reasoning_content`). So generation output is taken from `message.content`, and **regardless of what the API claims**, the raw string is re-validated by the existing `_decode()` (strict typing, `derived is True`, finite `0..1` confidence, exact field set) then by `_supported()` grounding — i.e. `extract()` already fails closed on malformed/unbacked output. `response_format` is an *optimization*, never trusted as a guarantee.
- If `response_format` is rejected by the gateway (e.g. `400`), that surfaces as a generation failure → `extract()` returns `_empty()`; no bypass.

**3. Pre-generation token budget (defensible, no fake ratios)**
- Chat Completions has **no** reliable preflight count endpoint on this gateway (and we must not call the Responses counter). So `count_input_tokens` must produce a **conservative upper bound**, not an estimate dressed as exact:
  - Compute `prompt_bound = canonical_utf8_bytes_of(instructions + document + schema_json)` — i.e. **1 token per byte** as a strict upper bound on tokenizers that use ≥1 byte/token for these ASCII-heavy strings. This is a *bound*, not an estimate.
  - Require `prompt_bound + MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS + RESPONSE_FORMAT_OVERHEAD ≤ MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS` (3000). `RESPONSE_FORMAT_OVERHEAD` covers the schema/format scaffolding in the request.
  - `generate()` then uses `max_tokens=MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` (300) and, after the call, verifies the **provider-reported** `usage.total_tokens ≤ input_bound + MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS`; if exceeded or missing → fail closed (same discipline as today's `extractor` usage check).
- **Honest limitation:** a 1-byte→1-token bound is deliberately loose (over-counts) to stay safe; it is *not* billed usage. Actual cost/usage is only the provider-reported `usage.total_tokens`. If you require an *exact* pre-call count, **this gateway cannot support it** — I will report that limitation rather than fake it. The hard cap is still enforced pre-generation via the byte upper bound, so checks are not weakened; they may simply block slightly-early on large documents.

**4. Offline tests** (`test/offline/ai/test_member3.py` additions; mock client, no network) — cover:
- **Well-formed** response → fields extracted.
- **Malformed responses:** non-JSON, wrapped in fences, unknown extra key, wrong types, duplicate keys, `derived=false`, `NaN` → all `_empty()` (fail-closed).
- **Unsupported feature:** `response_format` rejected (`BadRequestError`) and endpoint `405` → `_empty()`, no crash.
- **Token limits:** bound over budget → `generate` not called; provider usage over bound or missing → `_empty()`; exact-boundary pass.
- **Timeouts:** `count`/`generate` raising `TimeoutError` → `_empty()` (mirrors the existing REQ-COST-001 tests).
- **Provider-reported usage:** assert actual `usage.total_tokens` is what gets validated/logged; log lines contain token count but **not** the document text.
- **Adapter shape:** request uses `messages`, `response_format` json_schema strict, `max_tokens=300`, `max_retries=0`.

**5. Opt-in synthetic live test** (`test/live/ai/`) — one extra file, skipped unless `RUN_AZURE_LIVE=1`, uses **only** the same synthetic certificate, **one** `extract()` call, `max_retries=0`, reads creds from `~/CS5351/Project/agent.env` without printing them, and **reports** (not asserts success) values, model, prompt version, and actual `usage.total_tokens`.

## Scope & compliance
- Files: **new** `src/ai/cityu_provider.py`; **append** tests to `test/offline/ai/test_member3.py`; **new** `test/live/ai/test_member3_live_cityu.py`. `OpenAIProvider`, `extract()`, `schemas.py`, `requirements.txt`, teammates' files: **untouched**. No commit/push.

**Open question before I start:** the token bound — acceptable to use the conservative **1-byte→1-token upper bound** (safe but over-counts, may block early), or do you prefer I derive a tighter bound? I won't use an unverified char ratio. Awaiting your approval (批准).

```
    api_key  = <from ~/CS5351/Project/agent.env, never printed>,
    max_retries = 0,
    timeout  = 15.0,
)

    messages    = [
        {"role": "system", "content": <prompt_v1.0 instructions>},
        {"role": "user",   "content": "Hospital: City Clinic\nVisit date: 2026-10-21\nRecommended rest: 3 days\nDiagnosis: acute migraine"},
    ],
    temperature = 0,
    max_tokens  = 300,
)
```

**What changes from the prior attempt:** the `response_format` field is **removed entirely**. JSON is requested **only in the prompt** — I'll append a short instruction to the system message: *"Respond with only a JSON object of the form {\"hospital_name\":{\"value\",\"derived\",\"confidence\"}, …} for the four fields."* I will **not** assume `json_object` or tool-calling works; neither is used.

### Output limit explanation
- `max_tokens = 300` is the smallest supported limit suitable for a four-field JSON object of this size (each field: a short string or `null`, `derived: true`, a confidence number). That comfortably fits well under 300 tokens; the extraction needs only tens of tokens.
- On this model AssP1 observed answers arriving in `reasoning_content`, so `max_tokens` may also bound reasoning. That risks truncation — but per your rule I do **not** raise the limit or retry. If `finish_reason == "length"` truncates the JSON, it fails validation and the test fails honestly.

### Output handling & validation (unchanged strictness)
- The final answer is read **only** from `message.content`. If `content` is empty/None, the result is `_empty()` — I do **not** parse `reasoning_content` as the extraction answer.
- Whatever text comes back goes through the **existing** `extract()` pipeline unchanged:
  - `_decode()`: must be a dict with exactly the four fields; each exactly `{value, derived, confidence}`; `derived is True`; `value` is `str` or `None`; `confidence` numeric, finite, `0..1`. Any deviation → `_empty()`.
  - `_supported()`: each non-null value must match a unique `Label: value` line in the certificate (grounding).
  - low confidence (`< 0.75`) → value nulled.
- I'll also defensively strip a possible ```` ```json ```` fence before `_decode` **only if** present? — No: the existing pipeline already rejects fences (there's an offline test for it). To keep behavior identical to production, I will **not** add fence-stripping. If the model wraps in fences, it fails closed. (If you'd prefer minimal fence tolerance as an experiment, say so; default = no tolerance.)

- If `usage` is missing → reported as **unknown** (never zero).

- `hospital_name == "City Clinic"`, `visit_date == "2026-10-21"`, `rest_days == "3 days"`, `diagnosis == "acute migraine"`.
- All four `derived is True` and `confidence ≥ 0.75`.
- Any `None`/wrong value → **FAIL**.

````text
1: """Experimental Chat Completions adapter for the CityUCS/DeepSeek gateway.
2:
3: Lives entirely under test/live/ai/ as an experiment. It does NOT modify
4: production code and does NOT claim to enforce the 3,000-total-token cap in
5: advance; REQ-COST-001 is NOT verified by this path. The pre-call figure is a
6: deliberately loose upper bound, labelled experimental, never compliance.
7:
8: Implements the same ExtractionProvider protocol so the real extract() and its
9: ExtractionResult contract are exercised unchanged.
10: """
11:
12: import json
13: from typing import Any
14:
15: from src.ai.extractor import MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS, MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS, PROMPT, _wire_schema
16:
17:
18: class ExperimentalCityUProvider:
19:     """One-shot Chat Completions provider. No retries. Token cap NOT guaranteed."""
20:
21:     def __init__(self, client: Any, *, model_version: str):
22:         self._client = client.with_options(max_retries=0, timeout=15.0)
23:         self.model_version = model_version
24:         self.observed: dict[str, Any] = {}
25:
26:     JSON_SUFFIX = (
27:         '\n\nRespond with only a single JSON object, no prose and no code fences. '
28:         'It must have exactly the keys "hospital_name", "visit_date", "rest_days", '
29:         '"diagnosis". Each value must be an object with exactly '
30:         '{"value": <string or null>, "derived": true, "confidence": <number 0..1>}. '
31:         'Copy values verbatim from matching labelled lines; use null when absent.'
32:     )
33:
34:     def _messages(self, instructions: str, document: str) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
35:         # JSON is requested via prompt only; no response_format / tool calling.
36:         return [{'role': 'system', 'content': instructions + self.JSON_SUFFIX},
37:                 {'role': 'user', 'content': document}]
38:
39:     def _payload(self, *, instructions: str, document: str, schema: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
40:         return dict(
41:             model=self.model_version,
42:             messages=self._messages(instructions, document),
43:             temperature=0,
44:             max_tokens=MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS,
45:         )
46:
47:     def count_input_tokens(self, *, instructions: str, document: str,
48:                            schema: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
49:         # EXPERIMENTAL loose upper bound (UTF-8 bytes of the full payload as a
50:         # proxy). Not a verified tokenizer count and not production compliance.
51:         serialized = json.dumps(self._payload(
52:             instructions=instructions, document=document, schema=schema),
53:             ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
54:         bound = len(serialized.encode('utf-8'))
55:         self.observed['prompt_upper_bound_bytes'] = bound
56:         return bound
57:
58:     def generate(self, *, instructions: str, document: str, schema: dict[str, Any],
59:                  max_output_tokens: int) -> tuple[str, int]:
60:         params = self._payload(instructions=instructions, document=document, schema=schema)
61:         params['max_tokens'] = max_output_tokens
62:         result = self._client.chat.completions.create(**params)
63:         usage = getattr(result, 'usage', None)
64:         message = result.choices[0].message
65:         # Final answer is read ONLY from content; reasoning_content is never used.
66:         content = getattr(message, 'content', None) or ''
67:         self.observed['finish_reason'] = getattr(result.choices[0], 'finish_reason', None)
68:         self.observed['reasoning_present'] = bool(getattr(message, 'reasoning_content', None))
69:         self.observed['usage_prompt'] = getattr(usage, 'prompt_tokens', None) if usage else None
70:         self.observed['usage_completion'] = getattr(usage, 'completion_tokens', None) if usage else None
71:         self.observed['usage_total'] = getattr(usage, 'total_tokens', None) if usage else None
72:         # extract() requires a positive int total; if usage is absent we cannot
73:         # fabricate one, so report it honestly and let extract() fail closed.
74:         total = self.observed['usage_total']
75:         if type(total) is not int or total <= 0:
76:             raise RuntimeError('provider did not report usable usage; unknown')
77:         return content, total
78:
````

# Plan: production CityU Chat Completions provider (Member 3)

## What is proven vs unknown (token budgeting — the crux)
**Proven by our probes/run:**
- Chat Completions on `https://llm.cs.cityu.edu.hk/v1` is reachable and authenticated (a 200 with usage `345 = 260+85`).
- Responses API + `responses.input_tokens.count` → **405** (route rejected).
- `response_format: json_schema` strict → **400** ("unavailable").
- The model returns valid JSON in `message.content` for our prompt; reasoning absent on the successful call (but present on other tasks per AssP1 — so must never be trusted as the answer).
- Provider reports `usage.{prompt,completion,total}_tokens` on success.

**Unknown / not yet established:**
- A **documented tokenizer** for `CS5351/DeepSeek-V4-Flash` (no `tiktoken`/`transformers` installed; DeepSeek tokenizer not published to us). ⇒ **no verifiable exact pre-count**.
- **Chat-template + JSON-instruction overhead** in tokens (server-side, undocumented).
- Whether the gateway enforces any **request-token limit** we can rely on (no docs; nothing observed).
- Whether `chat.completions` exposes any count endpoint (it does **not** — only `create/list/...`; SDK confirms).

**Conclusion for REQ-COST-001:** A defensible *pre-generation* hard cap **cannot yet be established** for this deployment. Per your rule, **the production CityU path will remain blocked for token budgeting** (it will not claim compliance and will not carry the experimental byte proxy). I will implement the adapter's `count_input_tokens` to **fail closed** (raise/return an un-meterable sentinel) so `extract()` returns `_empty()` and never calls `generate`. The adapter and its offline tests are still built, but **generation is disabled for production** until the missing capability is supplied.

**Exact missing capability:** one of —
(a) a supported token-count endpoint for this gateway/model **on a Chat Completions route**, or
(b) the deployment's official tokenizer + documented chat-template/response-format token overhead, or
(c) provider documentation of an enforceable request-token limit we can query.
Absent one of these, I will not present any estimate as a guaranteed count.

> If you want the production path to actually generate, that requires relaxing this rule or supplying one of (a)/(b)/(c). Otherwise production stays blocked but correctly wired. I'll state this clearly in code docstrings and tests.

## 1. New production provider: `src/ai/cityu_provider.py` → `CityUProvider`
- Same `ExtractionProvider` protocol (`count_input_tokens`, `generate`). `extract()` and `schemas.py` untouched.
- `OpenAIProvider` stays as-is (kept available).
- `__init__(client, *, model_version)` → `client.with_options(max_retries=0, timeout=15.0)`; credentials supplied by caller (base_url/api_key), never logged.
- Requests (moved from experimental, verbatim logic):
  - `messages=[{system: instructions + JSON_SUFFIX}, {user: document}]`
  - `temperature=0`, `max_tokens=MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` (300), **no** `response_format`, **no** tool calling.
- Prompt-requested JSON; answer read **only** from `message.content`; `reasoning_content` ignored. If `content` empty → `''` → `_decode` fails → `_empty()` (fail closed).
- `generate` records `finish_reason`, presence flags, and `usage.*` to an `observed` dict; if usage missing/invalid → raise so `extract()` fails closed (usage unknown, not zero).

## 2. Consolidate logic; live test uses production adapter
- Move `ExperimentalCityUProvider` logic into `src/ai/cityu_provider.py`.
- Delete `test/live/ai/cityu_experimental.py`; update `test_member3_live_cityu.py` to import `CityUProvider` from `src.ai.cityu_provider`.
- The experimental byte-proxy `count_input_tokens` is **removed** from production (not carried over).

## 3. Local validation (unchanged, strict)
Reuse existing `extract()` pipeline verbatim: `_decode()` (exact 4 fields, exact per-field keys, `derived is True`, `value` str/None, finite numeric confidence 0..1) → `_supported()` grounding (unique labelled line) → confidence `<0.75` nulled. Invalid/truncated/unsupported/ungrounded → `_empty()`. No fence-stripping (matches existing offline expectation). No reasoning parsing.

## 4. Token budgeting (production path)
- `count_input_tokens` will **not** return a proxy. It will raise `TokenBudgetUnavailable` (new exception in `src/ai/cityu_provider.py`) → `extract()` catch → `_empty()`. So production **blocks before generation** and never claims a cap.
- Documented in docstring: REQ-COST-001 unverified; missing capability listed; no bypass flag.
- Post-call (only relevant if unblocked later): validate provider `usage.total_tokens ≤ budget` and log count **without** document text.

## 5. Cost/safety invariants
- Simple-request / `ai_allowed` False / empty doc → skipped before any provider IO (already in `extract()`).
- No automatic retries (`max_retries=0`); output capped (`max_tokens=300`).
- Log only token ints + prompt version; never document text or credentials.

## 6. Offline tests (`test/offline/ai/test_member3.py`, mocked client, new additions)
For `CityUProvider` via `extract()`:

- **JSON validation:** valid content parses; fences, non-JSON, missing/extra keys, wrong types, duplicate keys, `derived=false`, `NaN`, truncated → `_empty()`.
- **Grounding:** value not present verbatim in doc → nulled.
- **Usage handling:** `usage` present → total returned/validated; `usage` None → `_empty()` (not zero).
- **Adapter shape:** `messages` present, `temperature=0`, `max_tokens==300`, **no** `response_format` key; `with_options(max_retries=0,...)` called.
- **reasoning_content ignored:** content-only; when content empty but reasoning present → `_empty()`.
- **Unsupported output path:** simulate `BadRequestError` from create → `_empty()`.
- Run existing AI offline suite for regressions: `python3 -m pytest test/offline/ai -q`.

- Update `test/live/ai/test_member3_live_cityu.py` to use `CityUProvider`. Gated by `RUN_AZURE_LIVE=1`.

- **Maximum live requests if approved:** 5 (one per certificate), no retries.

````text
=== member3-ai-draft README ===
# MedLeaveAssist — Member 3 standalone draft

This folder is not the team repository. `src/schemas.py` is an unchanged copy of the supplied contract for running the tests here. Do not overwrite the team's schema or any existing prompt/module files when integrating. Review and merge only the relevant differences; coordinate test integration with Member 5.

## Offline verification

Python 3.10+ with Pydantic 2 and pytest:

```sh
python -m pip install 'pydantic>=2,<3' pytest
python -m pytest -q test/offline/ai
```

The provider adapter test uses a mock client; no API keys, external traffic, or real medical records are needed. The course requires a fixed semantic evaluation set and team CI gates before deployment. Neither is verified here.

## Team handoff

- **REQ-FR-002 / REQ-INV-002 / REQ-INV-004:** `src/ai/extractor.py` returns the four existing `ExtractionResult` fields with `derived=True`. Local guards reject malformed JSON, unknown or duplicate keys, invalid types, unattested values and conflicting labelled evidence. The model is not connected to policy, storage or status writes.
- **REQ-FR-005:** `student_guidance(PolicyDecision)` lists missing items and actual rule IDs supplied by the caller; routine guidance uses templates, not a model.
- **REQ-FR-006:** `counsellor_summary()` formats dates, duration, student name, derived diagnosis and caller-supplied document status. The shared schema has no display name or verified completeness field. Obtain these in the authorized caller, pass them explicitly, and do not substitute `student_id` for a name or infer document completeness.
- **REQ-COST-001:** One generation maximum with a 300-token output allowance. Preflight includes the schema and prompt; generation is blocked if the count plus output allowance exceeds 3,000 or counting fails. The token-count endpoint itself is a separate API request (not a generation). For cases the authoritative caller identifies as eligible for a simple path, set `simple_request=True`; this skips both requests. The 1,500-token average still needs live measurement on a representative synthetic evaluation set.
- **REQ-ARCH-004 / REQ-SEC-001 / REQ-SEC-002:** Before setting `ai_allowed=True`, the core/security integration must attest that the caller is authorized and document text has been sanitized and redacted. This draft's flag is an integration precondition, not an authentication or sanitization mechanism. Do not pass raw uploads, URLs, names, application histories or student IDs to the provider. Render guidance and summaries as plain text or escape at the UI boundary.
- **REQ-GOV-001 / REQ-GOV-002:** Core/governance should take model metadata from the configured adapter's `model_version` and the exported `PROMPT_VERSION`, using the team's audit convention for skipped calls. Optional fields with `value=None` and low confidence must not be treated as successful extraction. Evaluate against authoritative evidence and use the agreed review path for unreadable/ambiguous required fields; no routing is done here.

## Runtime context and limitations

This component receives no history, retrieval or resumable state. Its context is the versioned system instructions, strict JSON response schema and current approved document text. The order is precheck → assemble → preflight → generate → validate. No truncation or best-effort compaction: a request that doesn't fit is skipped to avoid deleting relevant medical evidence. The prompt is written in the JSON subset of YAML 1.2. Runtime format changes should use a versioned file and trigger the team's semantic gate.

The initial evidence guard is deliberately narrow: English and Chinese labelled lines, ISO visit dates, and simple integer rest durations. It rejects unsupported formats instead of guessing. Images and PDFs require a team-approved text/OCR integration upstream; this draft does not itself fetch or parse attachments, or verify that a document is authentic. A model provider may only receive preprocessed text after the team's privacy review. The optional `OpenAIProvider` expects a Responses SDK with `responses.input_tokens.count`, a pinned structured-output model and a configured client. Live counting, model quality, latency, and compatibility have not been tested. Use synthetic documents for live evaluation.
````

````text
# Completion Token Usage & Cost

By default LiteLLM returns token usage in all completion requests ([See here](https://litellm.readthedocs.io/en/latest/output/))

LiteLLM returns `response_cost` in all calls.

```
from litellm import completion response = litellm.completion(            model="gpt-6-luna",            messages=[{"role": "user", "content": "Hey, how's it going?"}],            mock_response="Hello world",        )print(response._hidden_params["response_cost"])
```

LiteLLM also exposes some helper functions:

-   `encode`: This encodes the text passed in, using the model-specific tokenizer. [**Jump to code**](#1-encode)

-   `decode`: This decodes the tokens passed in, using the model-specific tokenizer. [**Jump to code**](#2-decode)

-   `token_counter`: This returns the number of tokens for a given input - it uses the tokenizer based on the model, and defaults to tiktoken if no model-specific tokenizer is available. [**Jump to code**](#3-token_counter)

-   `create_pretrained_tokenizer` and `create_tokenizer`: LiteLLM provides default tokenizer support for OpenAI, Cohere, Anthropic, Llama2, and Llama3 models. If you are using a different model, you can create a custom tokenizer and pass it as `custom_tokenizer` to the `encode`, `decode`, and `token_counter` methods. [**Jump to code**](#4-create_pretrained_tokenizer-and-create_tokenizer)

-   `cost_per_token`: This returns the cost (in USD) for prompt (input) and completion (output) tokens. Uses the live list from `api.litellm.ai`. [**Jump to code**](#5-cost_per_token)

-   `completion_cost`: This returns the overall cost (in USD) for a given LLM API Call. It combines `token_counter` and `cost_per_token` to return the cost for that query (counting both cost of input and output). [**Jump to code**](#6-completion_cost)

-   `get_max_tokens`: This returns the maximum number of tokens allowed for the given model. [**Jump to code**](#7-get_max_tokens)

-   `model_cost`: This returns a dictionary for all models, with their max\_tokens, input\_cost\_per\_token and output\_cost\_per\_token. It uses the `api.litellm.ai` call shown below. [**Jump to code**](#8-model_cost)

-   `register_model`: This registers new / overrides existing models (and their pricing details) in the model cost dictionary. [**Jump to code**](#9-register_model)

-   `api.litellm.ai`: Live token + price count across [all supported models](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/blob/main/model_prices_and_context_window.json). **Jump to code**


[This is a community maintained list](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/blob/main/model_prices_and_context_window.json). Contributions are welcome!

## Example Usage[​](#example-usage "Direct link to Example Usage")

### 1\. `encode`[​](#1-encode "Direct link to 1-encode")

Encoding has model-specific tokenizers for anthropic, cohere, llama2 and openai. If an unsupported model is passed in, it'll default to using tiktoken (openai's tokenizer).

```
from litellm import encode, decodesample_text = "Hellö World, this is my input string!"# openai encoding + decodingopenai_tokens = encode(model="gpt-6-luna", text=sample_text)print(openai_tokens)
```

### 2\. `decode`[​](#2-decode "Direct link to 2-decode")

Decoding is supported for anthropic, cohere, llama2 and openai.

```
from litellm import encode, decodesample_text = "Hellö World, this is my input string!"# openai encoding + decodingopenai_tokens = encode(model="gpt-6-luna", text=sample_text)openai_text = decode(model="gpt-6-luna", tokens=openai_tokens)print(openai_text)
```

### 3\. `token_counter`[​](#3-token_counter "Direct link to 3-token_counter")

```
from litellm import token_countermessages = [{"user": "role", "content": "Hey, how's it going"}]print(token_counter(model="gpt-6-luna", messages=messages))
```

### 4\. `create_pretrained_tokenizer` and `create_tokenizer`[​](#4-create_pretrained_tokenizer-and-create_tokenizer "Direct link to 4-create_pretrained_tokenizer-and-create_tokenizer")

```
from litellm import create_pretrained_tokenizer, create_tokenizer# get tokenizer from huggingface repocustom_tokenizer_1 = create_pretrained_tokenizer("Xenova/llama-3-tokenizer")# use tokenizer from json filewith open("tokenizer.json") as f:    json_data = json.load(f)json_str = json.dumps(json_data)custom_tokenizer_2 = create_tokenizer(json_str)
```

### 5\. `cost_per_token`[​](#5-cost_per_token "Direct link to 5-cost_per_token")

```
from litellm import cost_per_tokenprompt_tokens =  5completion_tokens = 10prompt_tokens_cost_usd_dollar, completion_tokens_cost_usd_dollar = cost_per_token(model="gpt-6-luna", prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens, completion_tokens=completion_tokens)print(prompt_tokens_cost_usd_dollar, completion_tokens_cost_usd_dollar)
```

### 6\. `completion_cost`[​](#6-completion_cost "Direct link to 6-completion_cost")

-   Input: Accepts a `litellm.completion()` response **OR** prompt + completion strings
-   Output: Returns a `float` of cost for the `completion` call

**litellm.completion()**

```
from litellm import completion, completion_costresponse = completion(            model="bedrock/us.anthropic.claude-sonnet-5",            messages=messages,            request_timeout=200,        )# pass your response from completion to completion_costcost = completion_cost(completion_response=response)formatted_string = f"${float(cost):.10f}"print(formatted_string)
```

**prompt + completion string**

```
from litellm import completion_costcost = completion_cost(model="bedrock/us.anthropic.claude-sonnet-5", prompt="Hey!", completion="How's it going?")formatted_string = f"${float(cost):.10f}"print(formatted_string)
```

### 7\. `get_max_tokens`[​](#7-get_max_tokens "Direct link to 7-get_max_tokens")

Input: Accepts a model name - e.g., gpt-5.6-luna (to get a complete list, call litellm.model\_list). Output: Returns the maximum number of tokens allowed for the given model

```
from litellm import get_max_tokens model = "gpt-6-luna"print(get_max_tokens(model)) # Output: 128000
```

### 8\. `model_cost`[​](#8-model_cost "Direct link to 8-model_cost")

-   Output: Returns a dict object containing the max\_tokens, input\_cost\_per\_token, output\_cost\_per\_token for all models on [community-maintained list](https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/blob/main/model_prices_and_context_window.json)

```
from litellm import model_cost print(model_cost) # {'gpt-6-luna': {'max_tokens': 128000, 'input_cost_per_token': 2e-07, 'output_cost_per_token': 1.2e-06}, ...}
```

### 9\. `register_model`[​](#9-register_model "Direct link to 9-register_model")

-   Input: Provide EITHER a model cost dictionary or a url to a hosted json blob
-   Output: Returns updated model\_cost dictionary + updates litellm.model\_cost with model details.

**Dictionary**

```
import litellmlitellm.register_model({        "gpt-6.1-sol": {        "max_tokens": 128000,         "input_cost_per_token": 0.000002,         "output_cost_per_token": 0.000012,         "litellm_provider": "openai",         "mode": "chat"    },})
```

**URL for json blob**

```
import litellmlitellm.register_model(model_cost="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/BerriAI/litellm/main/model_prices_and_context_window.json")
```

**Don't pull hosted model\_cost\_map**
If you have firewalls, and want to just use the local copy of the model cost map, you can do so like this:

```
export LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP="True"
```

Note: this means you will need to upgrade to get updated pricing, and newer models.
````

````text
# Supported Endpoints

LiteLLM serves chat, responses, embeddings, images, video, audio, realtime, rerank, vector store, file, batch, MCP, and A2A endpoints. Send a request in the OpenAI, Anthropic, or Google format, and LiteLLM calls any provider that supports it. Use the Python SDK or the AI Gateway. Most endpoints work in both.

## Which endpoints does LiteLLM support?[​](#all-endpoints "Direct link to Which endpoints does LiteLLM support?")

Search by name, path, or Python function, or select a category. Each card opens the guide for that endpoint.

All (45)Chat and responses (8)Embeddings, rerank and retrieval (7)Audio and realtime (5)Images and video (4)Documents (2)Agents and tools (9)Safety and routing (3)Files, batches and tuning (4)Tokens (3)

### Chat and responses

Send prompts to any model in the OpenAI, Anthropic, Google or Bedrock format.

POST`/v1/chat/completions`

### [Chat Completions](/docs/completion/input)[#](#chat-completions)

Call any supported model in the OpenAI chat format, with streaming, tools and structured output.

Any chat provider122 providersPython: completion()

POST`/v1/completions`

### [Text Completions](/docs/text_completion)[#](#text-completion)

Legacy OpenAI text completion format, bridged to chat models.

Any chat providerPython: text\_completion()

POST · GET`/v1/responses`

### [Responses](/docs/response_api)[#](#responses)

OpenAI Responses API for any model, with stateful sessions, tools and background mode.

Any chat providerPython: responses()

POST`/v1/messages`

### [Anthropic Messages](/docs/anthropic_unified/)[#](#messages)

Call any model in the Anthropic Messages format, so Claude Code and the Anthropic SDK work unchanged.

Any chat providerPython: anthropic.messages.acreate()

POST`/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent`

### [Google generateContent](/docs/generateContent)[#](#generate-content)

Call any model in the Google generateContent format, with streaming through streamGenerateContent.

Any chat providerPython: generate\_content()

POST · GET`/v1beta/interactions`

### [Interactions](/docs/interactions)[#](#interactions)

Google Interactions API for any model, with stateful conversations and streaming.

Any chat providerPython: interactions.create()

POST`/bedrock/model/{model}/converse`

### [Bedrock Converse](/docs/bedrock_converse)[#](#bedrock-converse)

Native Bedrock Converse calls through the proxy with LiteLLM keys, budgets and logging.

POST`/bedrock/model/{model}/invoke`

### [Bedrock Invoke](/docs/bedrock_invoke)[#](#bedrock-invoke)

Native Bedrock InvokeModel calls through the proxy with LiteLLM keys, budgets and logging.

### Embeddings, rerank and retrieval

Embed text, rank results, search the web and query vector stores.

POST`/v1/embeddings`

### [Embeddings](/docs/embedding/supported_embedding)[#](#embeddings)

Turn text into vectors with any supported embedding model in the OpenAI format.

47 providersPython: embedding()

POST`/v1/rerank`

### [Rerank](/docs/rerank)[#](#rerank)

Rank documents by relevance to a query, in the Cohere rerank format.

19 providersPython: rerank()

POST`/v1/search`

### [Search](/docs/search/)[#](#search)

One web search API over Perplexity, Tavily, Exa, Brave and other search providers.

Python: search()

POST · GET`/v1/vector_stores`

### [Vector Stores](/docs/vector_stores/)[#](#vector-stores)

Create, register and search vector stores on OpenAI, Bedrock, Gemini and more.

13 providersPython: vector\_stores.create()

POST · GET`/v1/vector_stores/{vector_store_id}/files`

### [Vector Store Files](/docs/vector_store_files)[#](#vector-store-files)

Add, list, read and delete the files inside a vector store.

Python: vector\_store\_files.create()

POST`/v1/rag/ingest`

### [RAG Ingest](/docs/rag_ingest)[#](#rag-ingest)

Upload, chunk, embed and store documents in a vector store with one call.

Python: rag.ingest()

POST`/v1/rag/query`

### [RAG Query](/docs/rag_query)[#](#rag-query)

Search a vector store, optionally rerank, and answer with an LLM in one call.

Python: rag.query()

### Audio and realtime

Transcribe speech, generate speech and stream live audio.

POST`/v1/audio/transcriptions`

### [Audio Transcriptions](/docs/audio_transcription)[#](#audio-transcriptions)

Convert speech to text with Whisper, Deepgram, Gemini and other speech models.

Python: transcription()

POST`/v1/audio/speech`

### [Audio Speech](/docs/text_to_speech)[#](#audio-speech)

Convert text to speech with OpenAI, Azure, AWS Polly, ElevenLabs and other providers.

Python: speech()

WS`/v1/realtime`

### [Realtime](/docs/realtime)[#](#realtime)

Stream live audio and text over a WebSocket, load balanced across OpenAI, Azure and more.

WS`/v1/live/sessions`

### [GPT-Live Sessions](/docs/live_sessions)[#](#live-sessions)

Run full-duplex GPT-Live voice sessions with gpt-live-1 over a WebSocket.

WS`/v1/realtime/translations`

### [Realtime Translations](/docs/realtime_translation)[#](#realtime-translations)

Live speech-to-speech translation with gpt-realtime-translate over WebSocket or WebRTC.

### Images and video

Generate and edit images and video.

POST`/v1/images/generations`

### [Image Generations](/docs/image_generation)[#](#image-generations)

Generate images from a text prompt with DALL-E, Imagen, Stable Diffusion and others.

30 providersPython: image\_generation()
````
